## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我看看某个代码仓库里挂掉的测试，修好了再告诉你。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner on this Mac. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos: I'd open a task, work it out, and report back when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 지금 계신 컴퓨터의 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 바로 맡아서 해드릴 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性のある順序どおりの通信」、UDPは「速さ優先で届くかどうかは保証しない通信」です。

TCPは届いたか確認して再送し、順番も揃えてくれます。UDPはそうした確認をせず、速く軽く送るだけです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla, es decir, para ponerla al día con la rama principal y ordenar tus commits sin alterar historia que otros ya tengan. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o compartida, porque reescribir commits publicados rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的，保证数据按序、完整送达；UDP 不保证这些，但没有连接和确认开销，更快、更轻。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch to tidy its commits before sharing it. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rewriting shared history breaks everyone else's copy.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database in an append-only file before the change touches the main data. After a crash, the database replays that log to recover committed work and discard anything half-finished.
```

  [sent to task rust_haswell] 用户原话："我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快"

背景：上次已经把冷构建从 14 分钟降到 6 分钟（sccache + mold 链接），增量构建还没动。

要做的：
1. 在 haswell 上测量当前增量构建的耗时（改一个文件后重新构建），找出慢在哪里（如增量编译缓存未命中、链接耗时、codegen 单元、依赖重编等）。
2. 针对增量构建做提速，例如调整 incremental/codegen-units 设置、确认 sccache 对增量场景是否生效、链接器配置等。
3. 不要影响已经达到的冷构建 6 分钟的结果；若某项改动会让冷构建变慢，说明取舍。

完成标准：给出改动前后的增量构建耗时对比（同一改动、同一机器测量），并说明改了什么。

不在范围内：不合并到主分支，不推送、不开 PR；只在现有分支上提交。
  [retitled task rust_haswell] haswell 上 Rust 构建提速（冷构建 + 增量构建）
  [also used tools] task_find
```

  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海，600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="lowest">还没记</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">车次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
function render(d) {
  var limit = (d && d.limit) || 600;
  var trains = (d && d.trains) || [];
  var under = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < limit; })
                    .sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!under.length) {
    var e = document.createElement('div');
    e.className = 'k-empty';
    e.textContent = '暂时没有 600 元以下的班次';
    list.appendChild(e);
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = '0 班';
    document.getElementById('lowest').textContent = '';
    return;
  }
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  document.getElementById('lowest').textContent = '最低 ¥' + under[0].price;
  under.forEach(function (t) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = t.no;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = t.seats != null ? '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张' : '余票未知';
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = '¥' + t.price;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
var v = ducter.get('trains');
if (v && typeof v.then === 'function') { v.then(render); } else { render(v); }
ducter.onChange(render);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"limit":600,"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest write throughput</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">Not run yet</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">The first night lands at 23:00</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<script>
function render(v){
  var runs = Array.isArray(v && v.runs) ? v.runs : (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var sv = document.getElementById('latest');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  list.textContent = '';
  var latest = runs[runs.length - 1];
  if (latest) {
    sv.textContent = latest.writes;
    sv.className = 'k-sum-value';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + latest.p99 + ' · night ' + latest.night;
  } else {
    sv.textContent = 'Not run yet';
    sv.className = 'k-sum-value k-none';
    sub.textContent = 'The first night lands at 23:00';
  }
  runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var m = document.createElement('div');
    m.className = 'k-row-main';
    m.textContent = 'Night ' + r.night;
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = 'p99 ' + r.p99 + (r.stalls != null ? ' · compaction stalls ' + r.stalls : '');
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = r.writes;
    row.append(m, s, val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(function (v) { render({ runs: v }); });
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":2,"writes":"181k/s","p99":"4.0 ms"},{"night":3,"writes":"182k/s","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

</div>

<script>
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const sum=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?"¥"+sum.toLocaleString("en-US"):"还没记";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    const left=document.createElement("div");left.appendChild(main);left.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(left);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+sum.toLocaleString("en-US"));
}
document.getElementById("add").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim();
  if(!what||!yuan)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
  document.getElementById("day").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

$('save').onclick = function () {
  var t = $('t').value.trim(), ml = parseFloat($('m').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  if (editing) {
    feeds.forEach(function (f) { if (f.id === editing) { f.t = t; f.ml = ml; } });
  } else {
    feeds.push({ id: 'f' + Date.now(), d: todayKey(), t: t, ml: ml });
  }
  store();
  resetForm();
  render();
  $('addbox').open = false;
};
$('del').onclick = function () {
  feeds = feeds.filter(function (f) { return f.id !== editing; });
  store();
  resetForm();
  render();
  $('addbox').open = false;
};
resetForm();
load();
ducter.onChange(load);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":"f1","d":"2026-11-25","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":"f2","d":"2026-11-25","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  .k-row{cursor:pointer;align-items:center}
  .k-tick{width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;border:2px solid var(--d-line,#ccc);display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;margin-right:10px;flex:none;color:var(--d-good,#2a7);font-weight:700}
  .k-row.done .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted,#888)}
  .k-row.done .k-tick{border-color:var(--d-good,#2a7);background:var(--d-good,#2a7);color:var(--d-card,#fff)}

</style>

<script>
var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
var landed = [];
function render(){
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  items.forEach(function(it){
    var isDone = landed.indexOf(it.id) >= 0;
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row' + (isDone ? ' done' : '');
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    w …   var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = it.title;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = it.sub;
    var text = document.createElement('div');
    text.style.flex = '1';
    text.appendChild(main);
    text.appendChild(sub);
    wrap.appendChild(tick);
    wrap.appendChild(text);
    row.appendChild(wrap);
    row.onclick = function(){
      var i = landed.indexOf(it.id);
      if (i >= 0) landed.splice(i, 1); else landed.push(it.id);
      ducter.set('landed', landed);
      render();
    };
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  var left = items.length - landed.length;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent = left + ' open';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = landed.length + ' of ' + items.length + ' landed';
  ducter.summary(landed.length + ' of ' + items.length + ' landed');
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){
  landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(function(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){
    landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
});
</script>
; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","sub":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft"}]}

  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2388 characters): <main class="k-app"><div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">这趟出差合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total">还没记</div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">记了第一笔就会合计</div></div><h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2><div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div><details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary><div class="k-field"><label for="trip">出差名称</label><input id="trip" class="k-input" value="北京出差 11/20–21"></div><div class="k-field"><label for="day">日期</label><input id="day" class="k-input" placeholder="11/20"></div><div class="k-chips"><button class="k-chip" data-cat="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" data-cat="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div><div class="k-field"><label for="amt">金额</label><input id="amt" class="k-num" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div><button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button></details></main><script>
const fmt=n=>"¥"+Math.round(n).toLocaleString("en-US");
const esc=s=>String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));
let cat="交通";
document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.cat;document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false"))});
function draw(){
const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
const tot=document.getElementById("total");
tot.className=xs.length?"k-sum-value":"k-sum-value k-none";
tot.textContent=xs.length?fmt(total):"还没记";
document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记了第一笔就会合计";
document.getElementById("list").innerHTML=xs.length?xs.map(x=>'<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.trip)+' · '+esc(x.cat)+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day)+(x.note?' · '+esc(x.note):'')+'</div><div class="k-row-value">'+fmt(Number(x.amt))+'</div></div>').join(""):'<div class="k-empty">还没有花销,记一笔就会出现在这里</div>';
if(xs.length)ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total));
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
const amt=Number(document.getElementById("amt").value);
if(!(amt>0))return;
const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
xs.push({trip:document.getElementById("trip").value.trim()||"出差",day:document.getElementById("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:amt});
ducter.set("items",xs);
document.getElementById("amt").value="";
draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店(望京) 两晚"}]}

  [partner ack line] 好，我让负责 TTL 测试的那个任务再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

请在 TTL 测试里再加一条测试，覆盖缓存项恰好到期那一刻的行为：
- 沿用刚才的假时钟，把时间推进到恰好等于 TTL 的时刻，断言此时该值已经过期（或按现有约定：到期边界上的行为要明确写出来，并与现有实现一致）。
- 再补一个前一刻（比到期少 1 毫秒/1 个单位）仍然有效的断言，把边界两侧都钉住。
- 不要用真实计时。
- 跑一遍相关测试，并重复跑若干次确认稳定，把结果告诉我。

范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。在你自己的分支上提交即可。

2. 补一个测试，覆盖 Safari 下的这条路径（或把现有测试扩展到 Safari 场景）。
3. 跑测试，把 Safari 验证的结果和测试输出一起报告。

完成标准：Safari 验证结果明确（通过或失败及原因），新测试通过，已有 3 个测试仍通过。

范围外：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。修改留在当前分支上。如果 Safari 环境跑不起来，直接说明卡在哪里，不要用 Chrome 结果替代。
